from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import os
import sys
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

import numpy as np

from machines.composite import CompositeMachine
from machines.machine_base import MachineConfig
from scratches.smoke_lexical_grounding import (
    LEX_PAIR,
    POOL6,
    ROUNDS,
    lane_l0_anchor as sp12_lane_l0,
    lane_zwilling,
)
from scratches.smoke_paradigm_ursprung import lane_l0_anker  # kommittete SP11-lane

SEED = 42
STEPS_PHASE = 6          # 6 HALTED-Schritte + 6 autonome LOOP-Schritte = 12
N_PROBES = 5
CANDIDATES = (None, "goedel", "turing", "wittgenstein", "combined")
SP12_JSON = "scratches/lexical_grounding_experiment.json"
LOG_PATH = "scratches/endogene_agency_experiment.log"
JSON_PATH = "scratches/endogene_agency_experiment.json"
GPU_STEER_ROTATION = ("goedel", "turing", "wittgenstein", "combined", None)


def round6(x: Any) -> Any:
    return round(float(x), 6) if x is not None else None


def make_composite(mode: str = "in_loop",
                   oracle: Any = None) -> CompositeMachine:
    """Frische Composite seed 42 (in_loop oder oracle, Canon-Pool POOL6)."""
    return CompositeMachine(MachineConfig(seed=SEED, mode=mode,
                                          params=dict(POOL6)),
                            oracle=oracle)


def learn_and_pin(machine: CompositeMachine) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """SP12-Spiel + WM-RNG-Pinning (Arm-Schicht, dokumentiert)."""
    protocol = machine.wittgenstein.lexicon_game(
        [LEX_PAIR], rounds=ROUNDS, noise_level=0.0)
    machine.wittgenstein._rng = np.random.default_rng(SEED)
    protocol = [dict(entry, resonance_after=round6(entry["resonance_after"]))
                for entry in protocol]
    return protocol


def fingerprint(machine: CompositeMachine) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Vollfingerprint des Composite-Zustands (JSON-stabil)."""
    t, w, g = machine.turing, machine.wittgenstein, machine.goedel
    lex = w.tokenizer.lexicon
    return {
        "digest": machine.digest(),
        "rng": {name: json.dumps(gen._rng.bit_generator.state, sort_keys=True)
                for name, gen in (("goedel", g), ("turing", t),
                                  ("wittgenstein", w))},
        "axiom_pool": list(t.axiom_pool),
        "basis": list(t.basis),
        "expansions": list(machine.expansions),
        "step": machine._step_index,
        "noise_level": w.noise_level,
        "schedule_index": w._schedule_index,
        "lexicon": {k: np.asarray(v).tolist() for k, v in lex.items()},
        "self_model": np.abs(g.self_model).sum().item(),
        "input_log_len": len(g.input_log),
        "experiment_history_len": len(w.experiment_history),
        "generated_rules_len": len(w.generated_rules),
        "halt_verdicts": list(t.halt_verdicts),
        "transition_count": t._transition_count,
        "trace_len": len(machine._trace),
        "gap_history": [round6(x) for x in machine._gap_history],
    }


def lane_l0() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Zero-Regression: SP11-lane + SP12-lane (beide importiert) + der
    SP12-Zwilling FELDWEISE gegen die KOMMITTETE JSON."""
    l0 = lane_l0_anker()
    l0_re = lane_l0_anker()
    s12 = sp12_lane_l0()
    with open(SP12_JSON, "r", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        committed = json.load(fh)["twin"]
    twin = lane_zwilling()
    fields_equal = {
        "verdicts_a": twin["verdicts_a"] == committed["verdicts_a"],
        "verdicts_b": twin["verdicts_b"] == committed["verdicts_b"],
        "basis_a": twin["basis_a"] == committed["basis_a"],
        "basis_b": twin["basis_b"] == committed["basis_b"],
        "basis_b_sizes": twin["basis_b_sizes"] == committed["basis_b_sizes"],
        "expansions_a": twin["expansions_a"] == committed["expansions_a"],
        "expansions_b": twin["expansions_b"] == committed["expansions_b"],
        "resolver_direct": twin["resolver_direct"]
        == committed["resolver_direct"],
        "batches_ground_plain": twin["batches_equal_grounded_plain"]
        == committed["batches_equal_grounded_plain"],
        "batches_a_b": twin["batches_equal_a_b"]
        == committed["batches_equal_a_b"],
    }
    return {"sp11": {"fields_ok": bool(l0.get("fields_ok")),
                     "first_detect_ok": bool(l0.get("first_detect_ok")),
                     "deterministic": l0 == l0_re},
            "sp12_lane": {"fields_ok": bool(s12["fields_ok"]),
                          "first_detect_ok": bool(s12["first_detect_ok"]),
                          "deterministic": s12["deterministic"]},
            "sp12_twin_fields": fields_equal}


def lane_purity_cpu() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Proben im gelehrten Lexikon/LOOP-Trägheitszustand (N=5) und im
    leeren Lexikon (N=3): Bitidentität je Probe."""
    machine = make_composite()
    learn_and_pin(machine)
    machine.step("spiegel", lexical_grounding=True)         # LOOP-Trägheit
    fp0 = fingerprint(machine)
    b_state: Dict[str, Any] = {"probes": [], "purity": True, "diffs": []}
    for i in range(N_PROBES):
        probe = machine.observe("spiegel", lexical_grounding=True)
        fpN = fingerprint(machine)
        same = fpN == fp0
        b_state["purity"] = b_state["purity"] and same
        b_state["probes"].append({"n": i + 1,
                                  "verdict": probe.invariants["verdict"],
                                  "lexical_ast": probe.invariants["lexical_ast"],
                                  "purity": same})
        if not same:
            b_state["diffs"].append({"n": i + 1, "keys": sorted(
                k for k in fp0 if fp0[k] != fpN[k])})
    plain = make_composite()
    fpA = fingerprint(plain)
    a_state: Dict[str, Any] = {"probes": [], "purity": True}
    for i in range(3):
        probe = plain.observe("spiegel", lexical_grounding=True)
        same = fingerprint(plain) == fpA
        a_state["purity"] = a_state["purity"] and same
        a_state["probes"].append({"n": i + 1,
                                  "verdict": probe.invariants["verdict"],
                                  "lexical_ast": probe.invariants["lexical_ast"],
                                  "purity": same})
    return {"b_state": b_state, "a_state": a_state,
            "axiom_pool_unchanged": b_state["probes"][0]["purity"]}


def cpu_bio(steps_phase: int = STEPS_PHASE) -> Tuple[CompositeMachine, Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Biografie: 6 ungesteuerte Schritte -> Lexikon-Spiel -> Pin ->
    6 autonome Schritte (CPU: Tie-Degeneration)."""
    machine = make_composite()
    halted: List[str] = [machine.step("spiegel",
                                      lexical_grounding=False).invariants["verdict"]
                         for _ in range(steps_phase)]
    learn_and_pin(machine)
    auto_rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    for _ in range(steps_phase):
        result = machine.step_autonomous("spiegel",
                                         candidates=CANDIDATES,
                                         lexical_grounding=True)
        auto_rows.append({"verdict": result.invariants["verdict"],
                          "lexical_ast": result.invariants["lexical_ast"],
                          "chosen_preset": result.invariants["autonomous"]
                          ["chosen_preset"],
                          "candidate_gaps": {k: round6(v) for k, v in
                                             result.invariants["autonomous"]
                                             ["candidate_gaps"].items()},
                          "gap": round6(result.gap)})
    meta = {"halted_verdicts": halted, "autonomous": auto_rows}
    return machine, meta


def lane_det_cpu() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    m1, bio1 = cpu_bio()
    m2, bio2 = cpu_bio()
    equal = bio1 == bio2
    return {"deterministic": equal,
            "digest_equal": m1.digest() == m2.digest(),
            "expansions_equal": m1.expansions == m2.expansions,
            "tie_datum": all(r["chosen_preset"] == "raw"
                             for r in bio1["autonomous"])}


def lane_causal_cpu() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """SP12 x SP13 (CPU): Voraus-Sicht + verlustfreie Restaurierung +
    reale Expansion 3 -> 5."""
    machine = make_composite()
    learn_and_pin(machine)
    fp0 = fingerprint(machine)
    probe = machine.observe("spiegel", lexical_grounding=True)
    fp1 = fingerprint(machine)
    seen = {"probe_verdict": probe.invariants["verdict"],
            "probe_lexical_ast": probe.invariants["lexical_ast"],
            "pool_restored": fp1["axiom_pool"] == fp0["axiom_pool"],
            "basis_restored": fp1["basis"] == fp0["basis"],
            "expand_restored": fp1["expansions"] == fp0["expansions"],
            "fingerprint_restored": fp1 == fp0}
    real = machine.step_autonomous("spiegel", candidates=CANDIDATES,
                                   lexical_grounding=True)
    return {"seen": seen,
            "real": {"verdict": real.invariants["verdict"],
                     "lexical_ast": real.invariants["lexical_ast"],
                     "basis_after": len(machine.turing.state()["basis"]),
                     "expansions": list(machine.expansions),
                     "autonomous": result_autonomous(real)}}


def result_autonomous(result: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """RAW-Fließkommawerte (die EXAKT-Bänder vergleichen ungerundete
    Floats; gerundete Sichten nur für die Anzeige)."""
    au = result.invariants.get("autonomous", {})
    return {"chosen_preset": au.get("chosen_preset"),
            "best_gap": au.get("best_gap"),
            "candidate_gaps": dict(au.get("candidate_gaps", {}))}


# -- GPU-Lanes ---------------------------------------------------------------

def gpu_context(checkpoint_base: str, checkpoint_machines: str):
    """Argon-Orakel-Kontext (Muster verify_machines.py:355-399)."""
    import torch

    from machines.machine_base import ArgonOracle
    from machines.verify_machines import _load_machines_model

    device = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
    model, tokenizer = _load_machines_model(checkpoint_base, checkpoint_machines)
    oracle = ArgonOracle(model, tokenizer, device=device)
    return torch, model, tokenizer, oracle, device


def bias_fingerprint(torch: Any, model: Any) -> List[Any]:
    return [layer.moe.gate.expert_bias.data.cpu().clone()
            for layer in model.model.layers]


def bias_equal(torch: Any, before: List[Any], after: List[Any]) -> bool:
    return all(torch.equal(a, b) for a, b in zip(before, after))


def lane_purity_gpu(ctx) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Purity UNTER steer-Rotation: je Probe ein anderer Kontext (incl.
    plain); Maschinen-Fingerprint + expert_bias-Tensoren bitidentisch."""
    torch, model, _tok, oracle, _dev = ctx
    machine = make_composite(mode="oracle", oracle=oracle)
    learn_and_pin(machine)
    machine.step("spiegel", lexical_grounding=True)
    fp0 = fingerprint(machine)
    b0 = bias_fingerprint(torch, model)
    probes: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    purity = True
    bias_ok = True
    for i, preset in enumerate(GPU_STEER_ROTATION):
        label = "raw" if preset is None else preset
        if preset is None:
            probe = machine.observe("spiegel", lexical_grounding=True)
        else:
            with oracle.steer(preset):
                probe = machine.observe("spiegel", lexical_grounding=True)
        fpN = fingerprint(machine)
        same = fpN == fp0
        b_ok = bias_equal(torch, b0, bias_fingerprint(torch, model))
        purity = purity and same
        bias_ok = bias_ok and b_ok
        probes.append({"n": i + 1, "preset": label,
                       "verdict": probe.invariants["verdict"],
                       "lexical_ast": probe.invariants["lexical_ast"],
                       "purity": same, "bias_restored": b_ok})
        if not same:
            probes[-1]["diff_keys"] = sorted(k for k in fp0 if fp0[k] != fpN[k])
    return {"probes": probes, "purity": purity, "expert_bias_restored": bias_ok}


def gpu_bio(ctx, autonomous: bool) -> Tuple[CompositeMachine, Dict[str, Any]]:
    """GPU-Biografie: 6 ungesteuerte Schritte -> Lexikon-Spiel -> Pin ->
    6 Schritte als step_autonomous (oder als plain, raw-Zwilling)."""
    _torch, _model, _tok, oracle, _dev = ctx
    machine = make_composite(mode="oracle", oracle=oracle)
    halted: List[str] = [machine.step("spiegel",
                                      lexical_grounding=False).invariants["verdict"]
                         for _ in range(STEPS_PHASE)]
    learn_and_pin(machine)
    rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    for _ in range(STEPS_PHASE):
        if autonomous:
            result = machine.step_autonomous("spiegel", candidates=CANDIDATES,
                                             lexical_grounding=True)
            au = result_autonomous(result)
        else:
            result = machine.step("spiegel", lexical_grounding=True)
            au = None
        rows.append({"verdict": result.invariants["verdict"],
                     "lexical_ast": result.invariants["lexical_ast"],
                     "autonomous": au, "gap": float(result.gap)})
    meta = {"halted_verdicts": halted, "loop_phase": rows}
    return machine, meta


def lane_autonomy_gpu(ctx) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Die Wahl-Aussagen (switch + dominance) + det-Doppellauf GPU."""
    m1, bio1 = gpu_bio(ctx, autonomous=True)
    m2, bio2 = gpu_bio(ctx, autonomous=True)
    det = bio1 == bio2 and m1.digest() == m2.digest()
    rows = bio1["loop_phase"]
    spread_all = True
    non_raw = 0
    dominance_exact = True
    for r in rows:
        au = r["autonomous"]
        values = [float(v) for v in au["candidate_gaps"].values()]
        # EXAKT (ungerundete Floats): spread > 0 und
        # result.gap == best_gap == min(candidate_gaps).
        spread_all = spread_all and (max(values) - min(values) > 0.0)
        if au["chosen_preset"] != "raw":
            non_raw += 1
        if not (r["gap"] == au["best_gap"]
                and r["gap"] == min(values)):
            dominance_exact = False
    _twin, raw_bio = gpu_bio(ctx, autonomous=False)
    raw_gaps = [r["gap"] for r in raw_bio["loop_phase"]]
    auto_gaps = [r["gap"] for r in rows]
    mean_auto = sum(auto_gaps) / len(auto_gaps)
    mean_raw = sum(raw_gaps) / len(raw_gaps)
    dominant = mean_auto < mean_raw
    return {"det_doppellauf": det,
            "spread_pro_schritt": spread_all,
            "non_raw_anteil": non_raw / len(rows),
            "mean_auto": mean_auto, "mean_raw": mean_raw,
            "dominant_strikt": dominant,
            "gap_dominance": dominance_exact and dominant,
            "bio": bio1, "raw_bio": raw_bio}


def lane_causal_gpu(ctx) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """SP12 x SP13 (GPU): Voraus-Sicht + Expansion 3 -> 5 UNTER der Wahl."""
    _torch, _model, _tok, oracle, _dev = ctx
    machine = make_composite(mode="oracle", oracle=oracle)
    learn_and_pin(machine)
    fp0 = fingerprint(machine)
    probe = machine.observe("spiegel", lexical_grounding=True)
    fp1 = fingerprint(machine)
    seen = {"probe_verdict": probe.invariants["verdict"],
            "probe_lexical_ast": probe.invariants["lexical_ast"],
            "fingerprint_restored": fp1 == fp0}
    real = machine.step_autonomous("spiegel", candidates=CANDIDATES,
                                   lexical_grounding=True)
    return {"seen": seen,
            "real": {"verdict": real.invariants["verdict"],
                     "lexical_ast": real.invariants["lexical_ast"],
                     "basis_after": len(machine.turing.state()["basis"]),
                     "expansions": list(machine.expansions),
                     "autonomous": result_autonomous(real)}}


def findings(l0: Dict[str, Any], purity: Dict[str, Any], det: Dict[str, Any],
             causal: Dict[str, Any], gpu: Optional[Dict[str, Any]]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

    ok_l0 = (l0["sp11"]["fields_ok"] and l0["sp11"]["first_detect_ok"]
             and l0["sp11"]["deterministic"]
             and l0["sp12_lane"]["fields_ok"]
             and l0["sp12_lane"]["first_detect_ok"]
             and l0["sp12_lane"]["deterministic"]
             and l0["sp12_twin_fields"] is not None
             and all(l0["sp12_twin_fields"].values()))
    rows.append({"id": "sp13_l0_anker",
                 "data": {"sp11": l0["sp11"], "sp12_lane": l0["sp12_lane"],
                          "twin_fields": l0["sp12_twin_fields"]},
                 "assert": ok_l0,
                 "note": ("Zero-Regression über importierte committete Lanes "
                          "+ feldeidentischer SP12-Zwilling")})

    ok_purity = (purity["b_state"]["purity"]
                 and purity["a_state"]["purity"]
                 and all(p["verdict"] == "LOOP" for p in purity["b_state"]["probes"])
                 and all(p["lexical_ast"] == "('identity', 'x')"
                         for p in purity["b_state"]["probes"])
                 and all(p["verdict"] == "HALTED"
                         for p in purity["a_state"]["probes"]))
    if gpu and "purity" in gpu:
        gp = gpu["purity"]
        ok_purity = ok_purity and gp["purity"] and gp["expert_bias_restored"]
    rows.append({"id": "sp13_snapshot_purity",
                 "data": {"b_state_probes": purity["b_state"]["probes"],
                          "a_state_purity": purity["a_state"]["purity"],
                          "gpu_purity": (gpu["purity"]["purity"]
                                         and gpu["purity"]["expert_bias_restored"])
                          if gpu and "purity" in gpu else None},
                 "assert": ok_purity,
                 "note": ("Vorblick sieht LOOP, fressen der axiom_pool NICHT "
                          "(User-Fund Turing-Snapshot); GPU: Bias-Tensoren "
                          "unter steer-Rotation restauriert")})

    ok_det = det["deterministic"] and det["digest_equal"]
    if gpu and "autonomy" in gpu:
        ok_det = ok_det and gpu["autonomy"]["det_doppellauf"]
    rows.append({"id": "sp13_det",
                 "data": {"cpu": {"deterministic": det["deterministic"],
                                  "digest_equal": det["digest_equal"],
                                  "tie_datum": det["tie_datum"]},
                          "gpu_det": gpu["autonomy"]["det_doppellauf"]
                          if gpu and "autonomy" in gpu else None},
                 "assert": ok_det,
                 "note": ("Autonome Läufe bitidentisch; CPU dokumentiert die "
                          "Tie-Degeneration als DATUM")})

    causal_ok = (causal["seen"]["probe_verdict"] == "LOOP"
                 and causal["seen"]["probe_lexical_ast"] == "('identity', 'x')"
                 and causal["seen"]["fingerprint_restored"]
                 and causal["real"]["verdict"] == "LOOP"
                 and causal["real"]["lexical_ast"] == "('identity', 'x')"
                 and causal["real"]["basis_after"] == 5
                 and causal["real"]["expansions"] == ["step1:LOOP"])
    if gpu and "causal" in gpu:
        g = gpu["causal"]
        causal_ok = causal_ok and g["seen"]["fingerprint_restored"] \
            and g["seen"]["probe_verdict"] == "LOOP" \
            and g["real"]["basis_after"] == 5 \
            and g["real"]["expansions"] == ["step1:LOOP"] \
            and g["real"]["autonomous"]["chosen_preset"] is not None
    rows.append({"id": "sp13_causal_loop_closed",
                 "data": {"cpu_seen": causal["seen"],
                          "cpu_real": causal["real"],
                          "gpu_seen": gpu["causal"]["seen"]
                          if gpu and "causal" in gpu else None,
                          "gpu_real": gpu["causal"]["real"]
                          if gpu and "causal" in gpu else None},
                 "assert": causal_ok,
                 "note": ("Wort -> Programm -> Voraus-Sicht -> Expansion 3->5 "
                          "unter der Wahl (SP12 x SP13 gekoppelt)")})

    if gpu and "autonomy" in gpu:
        au = gpu["autonomy"]
        ok_switch = au["spread_pro_schritt"] and au["non_raw_anteil"] >= 0.8
        rows.append({"id": "sp13_endogenous_switch",
                     "data": {"non_raw_anteil": au["non_raw_anteil"],
                              "spread_pro_schritt": au["spread_pro_schritt"]},
                     "assert": ok_switch,
                     "note": ("Echte Wahl statt Einheits-Preset; HALTED/LOOP-"
                              "Reaktion als Datums in bio1 geliefert")})
        ok_dom = au["gap_dominance"]
        rows.append({"id": "sp13_gap_dominance",
                     "data": {"mean_auto": au["mean_auto"],
                              "mean_raw": au["mean_raw"],
                              "det_doppellauf": au["det_doppellauf"]},
                     "assert": ok_dom,
                     "note": ("g_auto(n) == best_gap == min(candidate_gaps) "
                              "je Schritt exakt; Mittelwert strikt unter raw")})
    else:
        rows.append({"id": "sp13_endogenous_switch",
                     "data": {}, "assert": None,
                     "note": "GPU-Lane OFFEN (ohne --gpu nicht gefahren)"})
        rows.append({"id": "sp13_gap_dominance",
                     "data": {}, "assert": None,
                     "note": "GPU-Lane OFFEN (ohne --gpu nicht gefahren)"})
    return rows


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.splitlines()[0])
    parser.add_argument("--gpu", action="store_true",
                        help="GPU-Lanes (argon_1b_machines oracle-mode) fahren")
    parser.add_argument("--checkpoint_base", default="./argon_1b")
    parser.add_argument("--checkpoint_machines", default="./argon_1b_machines")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    env_gpu = os.environ.get("SP13_GPU", "") in ("1", "true", "True")
    want_gpu = args.gpu or env_gpu

    print("== SP13 — Endogene Agency: kontrafaktisches Probehandeln ==")
    print("Canon: composite.py:219-301, turing_machine.py:323-327,")
    print("       wittgenstein_machine.py:507-525, machine_base.py:502-516")
    print("Prereg: N_PROBES=5, STEPS_PHASE=6+6, seed 42; Wahl = argmin Composite-Gap")
    print(f"Modus: {'CPU+GPU' if want_gpu else 'CPU'}")

    l0 = lane_l0()
    purity = lane_purity_cpu()
    det = lane_det_cpu()
    causal = lane_causal_cpu()

    gpu: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
    offen = False
    if want_gpu:
        try:
            ctx = gpu_context(args.checkpoint_base, args.checkpoint_machines)
        except Exception as exc:   # noqa: BLE001 — OFFEN, kein stiller Fallback
            print(f"GPU-Kontext nicht verfügbar ({exc}) — GPU-Lanes OFFEN.")
            gpu, offen = None, True
        else:
            gpu = {"purity": lane_purity_gpu(ctx),
                   "autonomy": lane_autonomy_gpu(ctx),
                   "causal": lane_causal_gpu(ctx)}

    rows = findings(l0, purity, det, causal, gpu)

    print()
    print("BEFUNDE:")
    for r in rows:
        if r["assert"] is None:
            print(f"  {r['id']:<26} OFFEN")
        else:
            print(f"  {r['id']:<26} {'PASS' if r['assert'] else 'FAIL'}")
    print()
    print("NOTIZEN:")
    for r in rows:
        print(f"  - {r['id']}: {r['note']}")

    fails = [r for r in rows if r["assert"] is False]
    opened = [r for r in rows if r["assert"] is None]
    if offen:
        opened.append({"id": "gpu_kontext", "note": "GPU-Kontext fehlgeschlagen"})
    print()
    print(f"BILANZ: {len(rows) - len(fails) - len(opened)} PASS; "
          f"{len(fails)} FEHLGESCHLAGEN; {len(opened)} OFFEN"
          f"{', unverwartet' if fails else ''}")
    print("CPU-Tie-Datum: ohne Oracle sind alle Kandidaten-Gaps identisch;")
    print("die Wahl-Aussagen sind die GPU-Aussagen (oracle-mode).")
    print(f"JSON: {JSON_PATH}")

    payload = {"l0": l0, "purity": purity, "det": det, "causal": causal,
               "gpu": gpu, "findings": rows,
               "all_pass": not fails and not opened, "open": [r["id"] for r in opened]}
    with open(JSON_PATH, "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        json.dump(payload, fh, indent=2, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False)

    if fails:
        print("ROT — Bands verletzt (Daten, kein Band-Schieben): "
              + ", ".join(r["id"] for r in fails))
        return 1
    if opened:
        print("OFFEN — GPU-Lanes nicht gefahren (kein stiller Fallback).")
        return 2
    print("GRUEN — Endogene Agency: die Maschine waehlt ihren Subspace selbst.")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

SP11_SEEDS = [11, 12, 13, 14, 15, 16]
STEPS_SWEEP = 24
SEED_ZW = 21
FORCE_ZW = {13}
STEPS_ZW = 24
ZW_BAND_LO = 14
ZW_BAND_HI = 22
THRESHOLD = 2.0                      # Canon-Default (beide Signale)
GAP_EPS = 1e-6                       # machine_base.py:51
RATIO_TOL = 1e-12

FINDINGS: List[Tuple[str, str]] = []
NOTES: List[str] = []
# P4-Klassifikation (2026-10-26, Vorschlag A): die Ursprungs-Vorhersagen
# T3a/T3b nahmen die Turing-Sub-Gap-Serie als FREIEN Faktor an — widerlegt
# durch das Akkumulationsgesetz (Docstring P4-REGISTER). Ursprungs-Zeilen
# bleiben FAIL (P4: Einstufung statt Re-Decide); korrigierte Lesung als
# _p4-Verdicte mit VOR dem Rerun fixierten Bands.
ERRATA: Dict[str, str] = {
    "sp11_t3a_expansionfrei":
        "E1: 'kein Anlass, kein Echo' nahm die Turing-Sub-Gap-Serie als freien "
        "Zustandsfaktor an. Sie ist die Akkumulations-Währung "
        "t_n = |C_{n-1} - s_n|/(1 + s_n + 1e-6) (prediction = kumulativer "
        "Kontostand, turing_machine.py:234/:197/:250; GAP_EPS "
        "machine_base.py:51/:531): Leerlauf-Blätter (s_n = 0) schlagen "
        "C_{n-1} ungedämpft auf — die Paradigmen in expansion-freien Läufen "
        "(Erstrun: seed12/14/15/16) sind Akkumulations-Echos. Korrigierte "
        "Lesung: sp11_t3_dedrifted_echo_p4.",
    "sp11_t3b_turing_echo":
        "E1: dieselbe Ein-Faktor-Annahme — der Freeze auf t_vor (Mittel der "
        "Vor-Expansions-Serie) misst kein Echo eines freien Sub-Gaps, sondern "
        "eine Misch-Serie der Akkumulations-Währung; die Überlebenden folgen "
        "dieser Mischung. Korrigierte Lesung: sp11_t3_dedrifted_echo_p4 "
        "(Gleitfenster statt Mittel-Freeze, wittgenstein_machine.py:413-414).",
}


def mark(name: str, state: str, note: str = "") -> None:
    """Verdikt-Register: state in {PASS, FAIL, OFFEN}."""
    FINDINGS.append((name, state))
    if note:
        NOTES.append(f"{name}: {note}")


def round6(x: Any) -> float:
    return round(float(x), 6)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Canon-Replikation des Signals (composite.py:167-182 / goedel:229-239)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def signal_from_history(history: Sequence[float]
                        ) -> Tuple[Optional[float], bool]:
    """Exakte Replikation: am Beobachtungsschritt sieht das COMPOSITE-Signal
    die Serie der Schritte 1..n-1 (Append nach der Prüfung, :143); das
    GOEDEL-Signal die Serie 1..n (Goedel.step läuft vor :135)."""
    if len(history) < 10:
        return None, False
    recent = float(np.mean(history[-5:]))
    baseline = (float(np.mean(history[-20:-5])) if len(history) >= 20
                else float(np.mean(history[:-5])))
    ratio = recent / (baseline + GAP_EPS)
    return ratio, bool(ratio > THRESHOLD)


def replicate_flags(series: Sequence[float], offset: int) -> List[Tuple[Optional[float], bool]]:
    """(ratio, detected) je Beobachtungsschritt n=1..len(series);
    offset der Anzahl Gaps, die das Signal im Schritt N schon sieht:
    Composite-Signal: offset 0 (sieht Serie bis n-1), Goedel-Signal: offset 1."""
    out: List[Tuple[Optional[float], bool]] = []
    for n in range(1, len(series) + 1):
        history = series[:n - 1 + offset]
        out.append(signal_from_history(history))
    return out


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Harness (SP10-Muster, seedy generalisiert)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def make_composite(seed: int, force_ast: Tuple[Any, ...]
                   ) -> Tuple[CompositeMachine, Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Composite mit Zwangs-Haken am composite-step-Zähler (pending-Flag):
    die ERSTE Regel des Batch wird am Zwangsschritt durch force_ast ersetzt —
    ohne eine Zeile Maschinen-Code (wm.create_rule-Abdeckung, SP10-Muster)."""
    machine = CompositeMachine(MachineConfig(seed=seed, mode="in_loop",
                                             params=dict(POOL6)))
    harness: Dict[str, Any] = {"pending": False}
    wm = machine.wittgenstein
    original = wm.create_rule

    def patched() -> GeneratedSymbolicRule:
        if harness["pending"]:
            harness["pending"] = False
            rule = GeneratedSymbolicRule(force_ast)
            wm.generated_rules.append(rule)
            return rule
        return original()

    wm.create_rule = patched
    return machine, harness


def run_composite(machine: CompositeMachine, harness: Dict[str, Any],
                  steps: int, force_steps: "set[int]") -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Verlauf mit je-Schritt-Attribution: Sub-Gaps, beide Signale (goedel /
    composite), neue expansions-Einträge, Basis-Größe."""
    rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    trace: List[str] = []
    for step in range(1, steps + 1):
        if step in force_steps:
            harness["pending"] = True
        pre_expansions = list(machine.expansions)
        st = machine.step(None)
        inv = st.invariants
        new_exp = [e for e in machine.expansions if e not in pre_expansions]
        trace.append(st.trace[-1])
        rows.append({
            "step": step,
            "rule_name": inv["rule_name"],
            "verdict": inv["verdict"],
            "transitions": int(inv["transitions"]),
            "t_count": int(machine.turing.state()["transition_count"]),
            "gap": float(st.gap),
            "w_gap": float(inv["submachines"]["wittgenstein"]["gap"]),
            "t_gap": float(inv["submachines"]["turing"]["gap"]),
            "g_gap": float(inv["submachines"]["goedel"]["gap"]),
            "g_ratio": (float(inv["paradigm_signal"]["ratio"])
                        if inv["paradigm_signal"]["ratio"] is not None else None),
            "g_detected": bool(inv["paradigm_signal"]["detected"]),
            "c_ratio": (float(inv["paradigm_signal_composite"]["ratio"])
                        if inv["paradigm_signal_composite"]["ratio"] is not None else None),
            "c_detected": bool(inv["paradigm_signal_composite"]["detected"]),
            "new_expansions": new_exp,
            "basis_size": len(machine.turing.basis),
        })
    expansions = list(machine.expansions)
    verdict_exp_steps = sorted(
        int(m.group(1)) for m in
        (re.match(r"step(\d+):(LOOP|COLLAPSE|EXPLOSION)", e) for e in expansions)
        if m)
    para_steps = sorted(
        int(m.group(1)) for m in
        (re.match(r"step(\d+):paradigm", e) for e in expansions) if m)
    gap_series = [r["gap"] for r in rows]
    w_series = [r["w_gap"] for r in rows]
    t_series = [r["t_gap"] for r in rows]
    g_series = [r["g_gap"] for r in rows]
    return {
        "rows": rows, "trace": trace, "expansions": expansions,
        "verdict_exp_steps": verdict_exp_steps, "para_steps": para_steps,
        "gap_series": gap_series, "w_series": w_series, "t_series": t_series,
        "g_series": g_series, "basis_size": rows[-1]["basis_size"],
        "final_basis": len(machine.turing.basis),
    }


def rep_composite_flags(run: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[Tuple[Optional[float], bool]]:
    """T2-Replikation: Composite-Detect-Muster aus der finalen Gap-Serie
    (das Signal am Schritt n sieht die Serie 1..n-1)."""
    return replicate_flags(run["gap_series"], offset=0)


def counterfactuals(run: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """T3b/T3c — Gefrieren auf dem Vor-Expansions-Niveau, Datenebene.
    None, wenn der Lauf keine Expansion hatte."""
    first = run["verdict_exp_steps"][0] if run["verdict_exp_steps"] else None
    if not first:
        return None
    w, t, g = run["w_series"], run["t_series"], run["g_series"]
    cut = first - 1
    t_pre = float(np.mean(t[:cut]))
    g_pre = float(np.mean(g[:cut]))
    cf_t = [t[i] if i < cut else t_pre for i in range(len(t))]
    cf_g = [g[i] if i < cut else g_pre for i in range(len(g))]
    cf_b = [(w[i] + cf_t[i] + g[i]) / 3.0 for i in range(len(w))]
    cf_c = [(w[i] + t[i] + cf_g[i]) / 3.0 for i in range(len(w))]
    return {
        "first_expansion": first,
        "t_pre": t_pre, "g_pre": g_pre,
        "orig_detections": list(run["para_steps"]),
        "cf_b_detections": [n for n, (_, det) in
                            enumerate(replicate_flags(cf_b, offset=0), 1) if det],
        "cf_c_detections": [n for n, (_, det) in
                            enumerate(replicate_flags(cf_c, offset=0), 1) if det],
        "orig_flags": [r["c_detected"] for r in run["rows"]],
        "cf_b_flags": [det for _, det in replicate_flags(cf_b, offset=0)],
        "cf_c_flags": [det for _, det in replicate_flags(cf_c, offset=0)],
    }


def t2_check(run: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
    """Mechanik-Falsifikator: rekomputierte (ratio, detected) == aufgezeichnet."""
    rep = rep_composite_flags(run)
    for row, (ratio, det) in zip(run["rows"], rep):
        if det != row["c_detected"]:
            return False, f"Flag-Differenz am Schritt {row['step']}"
        if (ratio is None) != (row["c_ratio"] is None):
            return False, f"Ratio-None-Differenz am Schritt {row['step']}"
        if ratio is not None and abs(ratio - row["c_ratio"]) > RATIO_TOL:
            return False, (f"Ratio-Differenz am Schritt {row['step']}: "
                           f"{ratio!r} != {row['c_ratio']!r}")
    # Detect-Menge-Ableitung aus den expansions-Einträgen (Konsistenz):
    return True, "ok"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# P4-Runde — Akkumulationsgesetz + Dedrift (Vorschlag A); Bands fixiert
# vor dem Rerun. Canon: turing_machine.py:234/:197/:250/:293,
# machine_base.py:54/:531, wittgenstein_machine.py:413-414.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def accumulation_check(run: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """P4-Vorschlag A: t̂_n = |C_{n-1} - s_n|/(1 + s_n + 1e-6) gegen die
    aufgezeichnete Turing-Gap-Serie; C_{n-1} = kumulierte transitions-Summe;
    zweiter Strang: je-Zeiler-Kontostand (state()["transition_count"], :293)
    == kumulierte Summe der transitions je Schritt."""
    s = [r["transitions"] for r in run["rows"]]
    residues: List[float] = []
    c_prior = 0
    for n, s_n in enumerate(s, 1):
        t_hat = abs(c_prior - s_n) / (1.0 + abs(s_n) + GAP_EPS)
        residues.append(abs(t_hat - run["t_series"][n - 1]))
        c_prior += s_n
    counts = [r["t_count"] for r in run["rows"]]
    count_ok = all(c == sum(s[:i]) for i, c in enumerate(counts, 1))
    return {"max_resid": max(residues) if residues else 0.0,
            "residues": residues, "count_ok": count_ok,
            "c_series": [sum(s[:i]) for i in range(len(s))]}


def dedrift(run: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """P4-Vorschlag A: gleitende Prediction statt kumulierter Summe —
    ῡ_{n-1} = mean(s_{max(1, n-10) .. n-1}) (Präzedenz
    wittgenstein_machine.py:413-414, np.mean(history[-10:]) im
    Nicht-Orakel-Zweig; leeres Verlauf → 0.0); dedrifted Composite-Serie
    cf_d = (w + t_dedrift + g)/3, Detect-Menge aus cf_d (Offset 0)."""
    s = [r["transitions"] for r in run["rows"]]
    t_dedrift: List[float] = []
    for n, s_n in enumerate(s, 1):
        history = s[max(0, n - 10):n - 1]
        u = float(np.mean(history)) if history else 0.0
        t_dedrift.append(abs(u - float(s_n)) / (1.0 + abs(s_n) + GAP_EPS))
    cf_d = [(w + t + g) / 3.0 for w, t, g in
            zip(run["w_series"], t_dedrift, run["g_series"])]
    flags = replicate_flags(cf_d, offset=0)
    return {"t_dedrift_series": t_dedrift, "cf_d_series": cf_d,
            "dedrift_flags": [det for _, det in flags],
            "dedrift_detections": [n for n, (_, det) in enumerate(flags, 1)
                                   if det]}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Lanes
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def lane_l0_anker() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """L0-Replay gegen hermeneutik_experiment.json — inklusive der
    committeten Signal-Kolumnen (paradigm_detected == Composite-Flag,
    paradigm_ratio == Goedel-Ratio)."""
    committed = json.load(open("scratches/hermeneutik_experiment.json"))
    out: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    fields_ok = True

    for arm, force_ast, key in (("kripke", TAUTAST, "kripke"),
                                ("quus", QUUSAST, "contrast_quus")):
        machine, harness = make_composite(SEED_L0, force_ast)
        run = run_composite(machine, harness, STEPS_L0, FORCE_L0)
        cm = committed[key]
        verdicts_ok = [r["verdict"] for r in run["rows"]] == cm["verdicts"]
        final_ok = run["final_basis"] == cm["final_basis"]
        gaps_ok = ([round6(g) for g in run["gap_series"]]
                   == [round6(g) for g in cm["gaps"]])
        exp_ok = run["expansions"] == cm["expansions"]
        det_ok = ([r["c_detected"] for r in run["rows"]] == cm["paradigm_detected"])
        ratio_ok = True
        ratios_live = [r["g_ratio"] for r in run["rows"]]
        ratios_commit = cm["paradigm_ratio"]
        for idx, (a, b) in enumerate(zip(ratios_live, ratios_commit)):
            if a is None and b is None:
                continue
            if a is None or b is None:
                ratio_ok = False
                break
            if abs(a - b) > RATIO_TOL:
                ratio_ok = False
                break
        arm_ok = verdicts_ok and final_ok and gaps_ok and exp_ok and det_ok and ratio_ok
        fields_ok = fields_ok and arm_ok
        out[arm] = {
            "run": run,
            "checks": {"verdicts": verdicts_ok, "final_basis": final_ok,
                       "gaps": gaps_ok, "expansions": exp_ok,
                       "paradigm_detected": det_ok, "paradigm_ratio": ratio_ok},
        }
    first_detect_ok = all(
        out[arm]["run"]["para_steps"][0] == 11
        for arm in ("kripke", "quus") if out[arm]["run"]["para_steps"])
    fields_ok = fields_ok and first_detect_ok

    # Herkunfts-Attribution der kripke-Paradigma-Einträge (T1-Daten):
    attr = []
    for n in out["kripke"]["run"]["para_steps"]:
        row = out["kripke"]["run"]["rows"][n - 1]
        attr.append({"step": n, "c_ratio": row["c_ratio"],
                     "c_detected": row["c_detected"],
                     "g_ratio": row["g_ratio"], "g_detected": row["g_detected"]})
    out["kripke_attribution"] = attr
    out["fields_ok"] = fields_ok
    out["first_detect_ok"] = first_detect_ok
    mark("sp11_l0_anchor", "PASS" if fields_ok and first_detect_ok else "FAIL",
         "Feldvergleich kripke+quus inkl. committeter Signal-Kolumnen "
         "(paradigm_ratio = Goedel, paradigm_detected = Composite)")
    return out


def lane_sweep() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """6 unverzwungene Läufe (Seeds 11-16, 24 Schritte) — T1/T2/T3-Daten."""
    runs: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for seed in SP11_SEEDS:
        machine, harness = make_composite(seed, TAUTAST)
        runs[f"seed{seed}"] = run_composite(machine, harness, STEPS_SWEEP, set())
    return runs


def lane_zwang() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Seed-21-Paar: unverzwungener Zwilling + Zwang {13} — T4-Daten
    (+ Doppel-Läufe für die Determinismus-Prüfung)."""
    twin, harness_t = make_composite(SEED_ZW, TAUTAST)
    forced, harness_f = make_composite(SEED_ZW, TAUTAST)
    zw_twin = run_composite(twin, harness_t, STEPS_ZW, set())
    zw_forced = run_composite(forced, harness_f, STEPS_ZW, FORCE_ZW)
    forced2, harness_f2 = make_composite(SEED_ZW, TAUTAST)
    zw_forced_re = run_composite(forced2, harness_f2, STEPS_ZW, FORCE_ZW)
    return {"zw_twin": zw_twin, "zw_forced": zw_forced, "zw_forced_re": zw_forced_re}


def determinism_check(l0: Dict[str, Any], sweep: Dict[str, Any],
                      zwang: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
    """Doppel-Läufe bitidentisch: trace + gap-Serie + Sub-Serien + Flags."""
    machine, harness = make_composite(SEED_L0, TAUTAST)
    l0_re = run_composite(machine, harness, STEPS_L0, FORCE_L0)
    l0_first = l0["kripke"]["run"]
    same_trace = l0_re["trace"] == l0_first["trace"]
    same_gaps = l0_re["gap_series"] == l0_first["gap_series"]
    same_subs = (l0_re["w_series"] == l0_first["w_series"]
                 and l0_re["t_series"] == l0_first["t_series"]
                 and l0_re["g_series"] == l0_first["g_series"])
    same_flags = ([r["c_detected"] for r in l0_re["rows"]]
                  == [r["c_detected"] for r in l0_first["rows"]])
    if not (same_trace and same_gaps and same_subs and same_flags):
        return False, "L0-Wiederholung weicht ab"

    machine11, harness11 = make_composite(11, TAUTAST)
    s11_re = run_composite(machine11, harness11, STEPS_SWEEP, set())
    s11_first = sweep["seed11"]
    if not (s11_re["trace"] == s11_first["trace"]
            and s11_re["gap_series"] == s11_first["gap_series"]
            and [r["c_detected"] for r in s11_re["rows"]]
            == [r["c_detected"] for r in s11_first["rows"]]):
        return False, "Sweep-seed11-Wiederholung weicht ab"

    if not (zwang["zw_forced_re"]["trace"] == zwang["zw_forced"]["trace"]
            and zwang["zw_forced_re"]["gap_series"] == zwang["zw_forced"]["gap_series"]
            and [r["c_detected"] for r in zwang["zw_forced_re"]["rows"]]
            == [r["c_detected"] for r in zwang["zw_forced"]["rows"]]):
        return False, "Zwang-Wiederholung weicht ab"
    return True, "ok"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# main
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def main() -> int:
    print("== SP11 — Der Ursprung der Paradigmen ==")
    print("Canon: composite.py:132-140/:167-182, goedel_machine.py:229-239,")
    print("       turing_machine.py:144-157 (loop_detector-Reset), GAP_EPS 1e-6")
    print("P4: turing_machine.py:197/:234/:250/:293 (Akkumulationsgesetz),")
    print("       machine_base.py:54/:531, wittgenstein_machine.py:413-414")
    print()

    l0 = lane_l0_anker()
    sweep = lane_sweep()
    zwang = lane_zwang()

    # Determinismus
    det_ok, det_note = determinism_check(l0, sweep, zwang)
    mark("sp11_det", "PASS" if det_ok else "FAIL", det_note)

    # T1: Urheber — niemals goedel.detected
    all_runs: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {
        "l0_kripke": l0["kripke"]["run"], "l0_quus": l0["quus"]["run"],
        **{k: v for k, v in sweep.items()},
        "zw_twin": zwang["zw_twin"], "zw_forced": zwang["zw_forced"],
    }
    breach = [(name, r["step"]) for name, run in all_runs.items()
              for r in run["rows"] if r["g_detected"]]
    t1_ok = not breach
    mark("sp11_t1_urheber", "PASS" if t1_ok else "FAIL",
         f"Goedel-Verletzungen: {breach}" if breach else
         "kein Schritt mit goedel.detected in allen 9 Läufen")

    # T2: Mechanik — Replikation aus der Serie
    t2_fail: List[str] = []
    for name, run in all_runs.items():
        ok, note = t2_check(run)
        if not ok:
            t2_fail.append(f"{name}: {note}")
    mark("sp11_t2_mechanik", "PASS" if not t2_fail else "FAIL",
         f"Fehler: {t2_fail}" if t2_fail
         else "Detect-Menge und Ratio je Lauf exakt aus der Gap-Serie rekomputiert")

    # T3a: Expansion-freie Läufe paradigmfrei
    free_runs = {n: r for n, r in all_runs.items() if not r["verdict_exp_steps"]}
    if free_runs:
        viol = [(n, r["para_steps"]) for n, r in free_runs.items() if r["para_steps"]]
        t3a_state = "PASS" if not viol else "FAIL"
        t3a_note = (f"Expansion-frei: {sorted(free_runs)}; Verletzungen: {viol}"
                    if viol else
                    f"Expansion-freie Läufe {sorted(free_runs)} alle paradigmfrei")
    else:
        t3a_state = "OFFEN"
        t3a_note = ("kein Expansion-freier Lauf in der Grundmenge "
                    "(6 Sweep-Seeds + Zwang-Zwilling) — Test nicht anwendbar")
    mark("sp11_t3a_expansionfrei", t3a_state, t3a_note)

    # T3b/T3c: Counterfaktur je Lauf mit Expansion
    cfs: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
    t3b_fail: List[str] = []
    t3c_fail: List[str] = []
    for name, run in all_runs.items():
        cf = counterfactuals(run)
        if cf is None:
            continue
        cfs[name] = cf
        if cf["cf_b_detections"]:
            t3b_fail.append(f"{name}: Überlebende {cf['cf_b_detections']}")
        if cf["cf_c_detections"] != cf["orig_detections"]:
            t3c_fail.append(f"{name}: {cf['cf_c_detections']} != "
                            f"{cf['orig_detections']}")
    t3b_state = "PASS" if (not t3b_fail and cfs) else ("FAIL" if t3b_fail else "OFFEN")
    t3c_state = "PASS" if (not t3c_fail and cfs) else ("FAIL" if t3c_fail else "OFFEN")
    mark("sp11_t3b_turing_echo", t3b_state, f"Fehler: {t3b_fail}" if t3b_fail
         else f"alle {len(cfs)} Gegenproben ohne überlebende Detection")
    mark("sp11_t3c_goedel_vergleich", t3c_state, f"Fehler: {t3c_fail}" if t3c_fail
         else f"Detection-Mengen in {len(cfs)} Gegenproben unverändert")

    # T4: Zwang
    induced = sorted(set(zwang["zw_forced"]["para_steps"])
                     - set(zwang["zw_twin"]["para_steps"]))
    in_band = [n for n in induced if ZW_BAND_LO <= n <= ZW_BAND_HI]
    mark("sp11_t4_forced", "PASS" if in_band else "FAIL",
         f"induzierte Paradigmas {induced}; in Band [14, 22]: {in_band}")

    # P4-Runde (Vorschlag A): Akkumulationsgesetz + Dedrift — Bands fixiert
    # vor dem Rerun; die Ursprungs-Verdikte t3a/t3b bleiben FAIL (ERRATA).
    free_names = [n for n, run in all_runs.items()
                  if not run["verdict_exp_steps"]]
    acc: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
    acc_fails: List[str] = []
    for name, run in all_runs.items():
        a = accumulation_check(run)
        acc[name] = {"max_resid": a["max_resid"], "count_ok": a["count_ok"],
                     "c_series": a["c_series"]}
        if not a["count_ok"] or a["max_resid"] > RATIO_TOL:
            acc_fails.append(f"{name}: count_ok={a['count_ok']}, "
                             f"max_resid={a['max_resid']:.3e}")
    acc_max = max((v["max_resid"] for v in acc.values()), default=0.0)
    mark("sp11_t3_accumulation_law_p4", "PASS" if not acc_fails else "FAIL",
         f"Strang-Verletzung: {acc_fails}" if acc_fails else
         (f"t_n == |C(n-1) - s_n|/(1+s_n+1e-6) in allen {len(all_runs)} "
          f"Läufen; max Residuum {acc_max!r}; Kontostand == kumulierte "
          "Summe in jedem Schritt (turing_machine.py:234/:197/:293)"))

    dd: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
    drift_fails: List[str] = []
    for name, run in all_runs.items():
        d = dedrift(run)
        dd[name] = d
        if name in free_names and d["dedrift_detections"]:
            drift_fails.append(f"{name}: überlebende {d['dedrift_detections']}")
    mark("sp11_t3_dedrifted_echo_p4", "PASS" if not drift_fails else "FAIL",
         f"überlebende Dedrift-Detections: {drift_fails}" if drift_fails else
         ("dedrifted Detect-Menge == leer in den expansion-freien Läufen "
          f"{free_names} (Gleitfenster-Präzedenz "
          "wittgenstein_machine.py:413-414); Rest-Läufe als Daten"))

    # Befunde
    print("BEFUNDE:")
    for name, state in FINDINGS:
        print(f"  {name:<26s} {state}")
    print()
    if NOTES:
        print("NOTIZEN:")
        for n in NOTES:
            print(f"  - {n}")
        print()

    if ERRATA:
        print("ERRATA (P4-Klassifikation):")
        for name, text in ERRATA.items():
            print(f"  - {name}: {text}")
        print()

    failed = [n for n, s in FINDINGS if s == "FAIL" and n not in ERRATA]
    offen = [n for n, s in FINDINGS if s == "OFFEN"]
    passes = [n for n, s in FINDINGS if s == "PASS"]
    print(f"BILANZ: {len(passes)} PASS; {len(failed)} FEHLGESCHLAGEN (unverwartet); "
          f"{len([n for n, s in FINDINGS if s == 'FAIL']) - len(failed)} dokumentierte Errata; "
          f"{len(offen)} OFFEN")

    payload = {
        "l0": {"kripke": {"checks": l0["kripke"]["checks"],
                          "expansions": l0["kripke"]["run"]["expansions"],
                          "para_steps": l0["kripke"]["run"]["para_steps"],
                          "transitions": [r["transitions"] for r in
                                          l0["kripke"]["run"]["rows"]],
                          "t_count": [r["t_count"] for r in
                                      l0["kripke"]["run"]["rows"]],
                          "final_basis": l0["kripke"]["run"]["final_basis"]},
               "quus": {"checks": l0["quus"]["checks"],
                        "expansions": l0["quus"]["run"]["expansions"],
                        "para_steps": l0["quus"]["run"]["para_steps"],
                        "transitions": [r["transitions"] for r in
                                        l0["quus"]["run"]["rows"]],
                        "t_count": [r["t_count"] for r in
                                    l0["quus"]["run"]["rows"]],
                        "final_basis": l0["quus"]["run"]["final_basis"]},
               "kripke_attribution": l0["kripke_attribution"],
               "fields_ok": l0["fields_ok"],
               "first_detect_ok": l0["first_detect_ok"]},
        "sweep": {name: {
            "verdicts": [r["verdict"] for r in run["rows"]],
            "expansions": run["expansions"],
            "para_steps": run["para_steps"],
            "verdict_exp_steps": run["verdict_exp_steps"],
            "gap_series": run["gap_series"],
            "w_series": run["w_series"], "t_series": run["t_series"],
            "g_series": run["g_series"],
            "transitions": [r["transitions"] for r in run["rows"]],
            "t_count": [r["t_count"] for r in run["rows"]],
            "c_ratio": [r["c_ratio"] for r in run["rows"]],
            "g_ratio": [r["g_ratio"] for r in run["rows"]],
            "c_detected": [r["c_detected"] for r in run["rows"]],
            "g_detected": [r["g_detected"] for r in run["rows"]],
            "basis_size": run["basis_size"],
        } for name, run in sweep.items()},
        "zwang": {name: {
            "verdicts": [r["verdict"] for r in run["rows"]],
            "expansions": run["expansions"],
            "para_steps": run["para_steps"],
            "gap_series": run["gap_series"],
            "w_series": run["w_series"], "g_series": run["g_series"],
            "t_series": run["t_series"],
            "transitions": [r["transitions"] for r in run["rows"]],
            "t_count": [r["t_count"] for r in run["rows"]],
            "c_detected": [r["c_detected"] for r in run["rows"]],
        } for name, run in
            (("zw_twin", zwang["zw_twin"]), ("zw_forced", zwang["zw_forced"]))},
        "counterfactuals": cfs,
        "p4": {
            "errata": ERRATA,
            "accumulation": acc,
            "dedrift": {name: {"t_dedrift_series": d["t_dedrift_series"],
                               "cf_d_series": d["cf_d_series"],
                               "dedrift_flags": d["dedrift_flags"],
                               "dedrift_detections": d["dedrift_detections"]}
                        for name, d in dd.items()},
            "expansion_freie_laeufe": free_names,
        },
        "findings": [{"name": n, "state": s} for n, s in FINDINGS],
        "notes": NOTES,
        "errata": ERRATA,
        "all_pass": not failed,
        "open": offen,
    }
    with open("scratches/paradigm_ursprung_experiment.json", "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        json.dump(payload, fh, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, sort_keys=False)
    print("JSON: scratches/paradigm_ursprung_experiment.json")

    if failed:
        print(f"FEHLGESCHLAGEN: {failed}")
        return 1
    print(f"GRUEN — Loesungskette geschlossen (OFFEN: {offen if offen else 'keine'}).")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

"""
smoke_lexical_grounding.py — SP12: Lexical Grounding — das gelernte Wort
wird Programm (Semantische Kausalität; erster Stein des geschlossenen
Subjekt-Zyklus, Nordstern-Nachricht 2026-10-26, Task 1).

Canon: composite.py:108-140 (step / text-Fluss :110 / Bridging :120-124),
:66-92 (identity-Tautologie -> Selbst-Transition -> LOOP),
turing_machine.py:144-157 (expand_basis: EIN Term je Call) und :241-246
(Turing INNERER Expand je Halt-Fall => +2 Terme je LOOP-Schritt; Canon-Beleg
SP7-L0 final_basis 7 über 2 LOOP-Expansionen),
wittgenstein_machine.py:49-50 (SymbolicOperator.UNARY/BINARY),
:152-246 (embed_word lexikon-first :176, resonance :214, learn_pair :218,
word_formula :163), :342-381 (lexicon_game: Lernen nur bei success >= 0.5),
composite.py (SP12-Edits: step(obj, lexical_grounding=False) +
WittgensteinMachine.resolve_lexical_ast — reine Lexikon-Lesung, kein RNG).

PREREG (P2-Freeze — fixiert vor dem Erstrun; Vor-Smokes 2026-10-26 sind im
Plan ~/.claude/plans/sp12-lexical-grounding.md dokumentiert — Änderungs-
notizen 1-3: Klang-Dach formel_max 0.9831, L0-Regression grün, +2-Expansions-
Mechanik).

Kernthese (User-Nordstern, Task 1): sozial gelernte Wörter übersetzen sich
in AST-Programme. Der Resolver ist reine Lexikon-Lesung: Kandidaten sind
AUSCHLIESSLICH Lexikon-gelernte Operator-Wörter (Klang-vs-Gebrauch-Scheidung
— der SH-72-Fallback löst NIE auf, siehe Klang-Dach-Datum), tau = 0.85
(User-Prereg), UNARY -> (op, 'x'), BINARY -> (op, 'x', 'x').

VERDICT-MAP (Bands fixiert VOR dem Erstrun):
  sp12_l0_anker — machines-Edit-Regression: die kommittete
    lane_l0_anker-Funktion (aus dem committeten SP11-Skript importiert)
    liefert fields_ok + first_detect_ok; das Doppel-Replay ist bitidentisch.
    Falsifikator: jede abweichende Feld-Serie.
  sp12_det — Zwilling-Doppel-Läufe (Arm A ×2, Arm B ×2) bitidentisch.
    Falsifikator: jede Abweichung.
  sp12_t1_semantik — (a) alle 5 Lernrunden learned=True (noise_level=0.0,
    Canon-Arg lexicon_game für Lern-Sicherheit); (b) resonance_after[4] >=
    0.85; (c) resolve_lexical_ast("spiegel") == ('identity', 'x');
    (d) NACH-Lexikon-Kandidaten == ["identity"]; (e) B-Schritt-1
    invariants.lexical_ast == "('identity', 'x')".
    Falsifikator: jede Bandverletzung (kein tau-Schieben).
  sp12_t2_rng_reinheit — generierte Batches TRIPLE-identisch (Arm A
    grounded, Arm A' plain, Arm B mit WM-RNG-Pinning nach den 5 Lernrunden):
    batch_asts + rule_names + w_series je Schritt gleich. Die Resolution
    ist reine Lexikon-Lesung — sie konsumiert KEINE RNG-Züge.
    Falsifikator: jede Batch-Differenz.
  sp12_t3_scheidung — (a) Arm A lexical_ast == None an JEDEM Schritt
    (kein Klang-Auslösen über den SH-72-Fallback); (b) Arm A == Arm A'
    (opt-in-Default-Invarianz im leeren Lexikon); (c) Klang-Dach als DATEN
    (formel_max/formel_argmax — SP2-Messkonvention, kein Band).
    Falsifikator: Verletzung von (a) oder (b).
  sp12_t4_verdikt — Arm A: ALLE Verdikte HALTED, final_basis == 3,
    expansions == []; Arm B: Verdikte[0:2] == [LOOP, LOOP]; Basis nach
    Schritt 1 == 5 (User-Pass „Basis auf 5"), nach Schritt 2 == 7
    (+2 je LOOP: Turing-innerer Expand :241-246 + Composite :132-134);
    expansions[0:2] == ['step1:LOOP', 'step2:LOOP']; Schritte 3-6 als
    DATEN (mögliche Pool-Erschöpfung wird dokumentiert, kein Band).
    Falsifikator: Arm-A-Expansion, Arm-B-Verdikt != LOOP am resolvierten
    Schritt, oder fehlende Basis-Differenz.

Lanes: l0_anker (Regression), det, zwilling (A/A'/B, seed 42, 6 Schritte,
obj="spiegel" je Schritt). CPU-only; kein Maschinen-Code-Zugriff über die
harness-Ebene hinaus (die SP12-Extension der Maschinen ist committet und
durch lane_l0_anker regressions-geprüft).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Tuple

import numpy as np

from machines.composite import CompositeMachine
from machines.machine_base import MachineConfig
from machines.wittgenstein_machine import SymbolicOperator
from scratches.smoke_paradigm_ursprung import lane_l0_anker

SEED = 42
STEPS = 6
TAU = 0.85
ROUNDS = 5
LEX_PAIR = ("spiegel", "identity")
POOL6 = {"axiom_pool": ["ALPHA", "BETA", "GAMMA", "DELTA", "EPSILON",
                        "ZETA"]}
LOG_PATH = "scratches/lexical_grounding_experiment.log"
JSON_PATH = "scratches/lexical_grounding_experiment.json"


def round6(x: Any) -> Any:
    return round(float(x), 6) if x is not None else None


def make_composite() -> CompositeMachine:
    """Frischer composite seed 42 (in_loop, Canon-Pool POOL6)."""
    return CompositeMachine(MachineConfig(seed=SEED, mode="in_loop",
                                          params=dict(POOL6)))


def run_lexical(machine: CompositeMachine, word: str, steps: int,
                lexical: bool = True) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Verlauf mit je-Schritt-Attribution: lexikale Resolution, generierter
    Batch-AST (RNG-Marker für T2), Verdikt, Basis-Größe, Sub-Gaps."""
    rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    wm = machine.wittgenstein
    for step in range(1, steps + 1):
        rules_before = len(wm.generated_rules)
        st = machine.step(word, lexical_grounding=lexical)
        inv = st.invariants
        batch_ast = (str(wm.generated_rules[rules_before].ast)
                     if len(wm.generated_rules) > rules_before else None)
        t_state = machine.turing.state()
        rows.append({
            "step": step,
            "step_index": st.step,
            "lexical_word": inv["lexical_word"],
            "lexical_ast": inv["lexical_ast"],
            "batch_ast": batch_ast,
            "rule_name": inv["rule_name"],
            "verdict": inv["verdict"],
            "transitions": int(inv["transitions"]),
            "basis_size": len(t_state["basis"]),
            "gap": round6(st.gap),
            "w_gap": round6(inv["submachines"]["wittgenstein"]["gap"]),
            "t_gap": round6(inv["submachines"]["turing"]["gap"]),
            "g_gap": round6(inv["submachines"]["goedel"]["gap"]),
        })
    return {"rows": rows,
            "verdicts": [r["verdict"] for r in rows],
            "lexical_asts": [r["lexical_ast"] for r in rows],
            "batch_asts": [r["batch_ast"] for r in rows],
            "rule_names": [r["rule_name"] for r in rows],
            "w_series": [r["w_gap"] for r in rows],
            "basis_sizes": [r["basis_size"] for r in rows],
            "final_basis": rows[-1]["basis_size"],
            "expansions": list(machine.expansions)}


def resonance_profile(machine: CompositeMachine) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Klang-Raum-Messung (DATA — Klang-Dach, SP2-Konvention) plus die
    NACH-Spiel-Lexikon-Resonanz."""
    tok = machine.wittgenstein.tokenizer
    word = LEX_PAIR[0]
    k, idx, kind = tok.word_formula(word)
    base = tok.embed_word(word)
    formel = {op: round6(tok.resonance(base, tok.embed_word(op)))
              for op in SymbolicOperator.UNARY + SymbolicOperator.BINARY}
    lexikon_ops = [op for op in SymbolicOperator.UNARY + SymbolicOperator.BINARY
                   if op in tok.lexicon]
    lexikon = {op: round6(tok.resonance(tok.embed_word(word),
                                        tok.embed_word(op)))
               for op in lexikon_ops}
    return {
        "spiegel_formula": {"k": k, "idx": idx, "kind": kind},
        "formel_achsen": formel,
        "formel_max": max(formel.values()),
        "formel_argmax": max(formel, key=formel.get),
        "lexikon_woerter": sorted(tok.lexicon.keys()),
        "lexikon_kandidaten": lexikon_ops,
        "lexikon_resonanzen": lexikon,
    }


def arm_a_grounded() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Arm A: Wort OHNE gelernte Bedeutung — Resolver None (kein Klang-
    Auslösen), normale Kette."""
    machine = make_composite()
    run = run_lexical(machine, "spiegel", STEPS, lexical=True)
    run["profile"] = resonance_profile(machine)
    return run


def arm_a_plain() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Arm A': OHNE lexical_grounding — opt-in-Default-Invarianz."""
    machine = make_composite()
    return run_lexical(machine, "spiegel", STEPS, lexical=False)


def arm_b_after_learning() -> Tuple[Dict[str, Any], Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Arm B: 5 Lernrunden (noise_level=0.0 — Canon-Arg für Lern-Sicherheit),
    DANACH WM-RNG-Pinning (Arm-Schicht: default_rng(42) — identisch zum
    reset-Body), dann der Verlauf: das gelernte Wort wird Programm."""
    machine = make_composite()
    protocol = machine.wittgenstein.lexicon_game(
        [LEX_PAIR], rounds=ROUNDS, noise_level=0.0)
    profile_b = resonance_profile(machine)
    machine.wittgenstein._rng = np.random.default_rng(SEED)
    run = run_lexical(machine, "spiegel", STEPS, lexical=True)
    return run, {"protocol": protocol,
                 "resonance_after": [e["resonance_after"] for e in protocol],
                 "learned": [e["learned"] for e in protocol],
                 "profile_b": profile_b}


def lane_l0_anchor() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """machines-Edit-Regression über die kommittete SP11-Lane (die
    committete Feldvergleich-Mechanik, unverändert)."""
    l0 = lane_l0_anker()
    l0_re = lane_l0_anker()
    return {"result": l0,
            "fields_ok": bool(l0.get("fields_ok")),
            "first_detect_ok": bool(l0.get("first_detect_ok")),
            "deterministic": l0 == l0_re}


def lane_det(arm_a: Dict[str, Any], arm_b_run: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Doppel-Läufe: Arm A und Arm B je frisch reproduziert, bitidentisch."""
    a_re = arm_a_grounded()
    b_re, _b_meta_re = arm_b_after_learning()
    return {"arm_a_deterministic": a_re == arm_a,
            "arm_b_deterministic": b_re == arm_b_run}


def lane_zwilling() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    a = arm_a_grounded()
    a_plain = arm_a_plain()
    b, b_meta = arm_b_after_learning()
    m_direct = make_composite()
    m_direct.wittgenstein.lexicon_game([LEX_PAIR], rounds=ROUNDS,
                                       noise_level=0.0)
    resolver_direct = m_direct.wittgenstein.resolve_lexical_ast("spiegel")
    return {
        "arm_a": a, "arm_a_plain": a_plain, "arm_b": b, "arm_b_meta": b_meta,
        "resolver_direct": str(resolver_direct),
        "verdicts_a": a["verdicts"], "verdicts_b": b["verdicts"],
        "basis_a": a["final_basis"], "basis_b": b["final_basis"],
        "basis_b_sizes": b["basis_sizes"],
        "expansions_a": a["expansions"], "expansions_b": b["expansions"],
        "batches_equal_grounded_plain": (
            a["batch_asts"] == a_plain["batch_asts"]
            and a["rule_names"] == a_plain["rule_names"]
            and a["w_series"] == a_plain["w_series"]),
        "batches_equal_a_b": (
            a["batch_asts"] == b["batch_asts"]
            and a["rule_names"] == b["rule_names"]
            and a["w_series"] == b["w_series"]),
    }


def findings(twin: Dict[str, Any], det: Dict[str, Any],
             l0: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    a = twin["arm_a"]
    apr = twin["arm_a_plain"]
    b = twin["arm_b"]
    meta = twin["arm_b_meta"]
    prof = a["profile"]

    # --- sp12_l0_anker ---
    rows.append({"id": "sp12_l0_anker",
                 "data": {"fields_ok": l0["fields_ok"],
                          "first_detect_ok": l0["first_detect_ok"],
                          "deterministic": l0["deterministic"]},
                 "assert": (l0["fields_ok"] and l0["first_detect_ok"]
                            and l0["deterministic"]),
                 "note": ("machines-Regression über die kommittete SP11-Lane — "
                          "die committeten Feld-Serien bleiben bitidentisch")})

    # --- sp12_det ---
    rows.append({"id": "sp12_det",
                 "data": {"arm_a": det["arm_a_deterministic"],
                          "arm_b": det["arm_b_deterministic"]},
                 "assert": det["arm_a_deterministic"]
                 and det["arm_b_deterministic"],
                 "note": "Zwilling-Doppel-Läufe bitidentisch"})

    # --- sp12_t1_semantik ---
    res_serie = meta["resonance_after"]
    learned_all = all(meta["learned"])
    nach_kandidaten = meta["profile_b"]["lexikon_kandidaten"]
    nach_resonanz = meta["profile_b"]["lexikon_resonanzen"]
    resolver_direct = twin["resolver_direct"]
    rows.append({"id": "sp12_t1_semantik",
                 "data": {"learned": meta["learned"],
                          "resonance_after": res_serie,
                          "resonance_final": res_serie[-1],
                          "resolver_direct": resolver_direct,
                          "nach_kandidaten": nach_kandidaten,
                          "nach_resonanzen": nach_resonanz},
                 "assert": (learned_all
                            and res_serie[-1] >= TAU
                            and resolver_direct == "('identity', 'x')"
                            and nach_kandidaten == ["identity"]
                            and float(nach_resonanz.get("identity", 0.0)) >= TAU
                            and b["rows"][0]["lexical_ast"]
                            == "('identity', 'x')"),
                 "note": ("5 Lernrunden (noise_level=0.0) — Gebrauchs-Achse "
                          "resonance_after[-1] >= tau; der Resolver löst "
                          "direkt UND im Lauf zu ('identity', 'x') auf")})

    # --- sp12_t2_rng_reinheit ---
    rows.append({"id": "sp12_t2_rng_reinheit",
                 "data": {"batches_a_plain": twin["batches_equal_grounded_plain"],
                          "batches_a_b": twin["batches_equal_a_b"]},
                 "assert": (twin["batches_equal_grounded_plain"]
                            and twin["batches_equal_a_b"]),
                 "note": ("Batch-ASTs + rule_names + w_series je Schritt "
                          "identisch in A/A'/B — die Resolution ist reine "
                          "Lexikon-Lesung (kein RNG-Zug)")})

    # --- sp12_t3_scheidung ---
    rows.append({"id": "sp12_t3_scheidung",
                 "data": {"vor_resolution": a["lexical_asts"],
                          "klang_dach_max": prof["formel_max"],
                          "klang_dach_argmax": prof["formel_argmax"],
                          "spiegel_formula": prof["spiegel_formula"]},
                 "assert": (all(x is None for x in a["lexical_asts"])
                            and twin["batches_equal_grounded_plain"]),
                 "note": ("Vor dem Spiel löst der SH-72-Fallback NICHT auf "
                          "(Klang-Dach als DATEN; die LEXIKON-Gegenwart ist "
                          "die Scheidung); opt-in-Default-Invarianz A == A'")})

    # --- sp12_t4_verdikt ---
    rows.append({"id": "sp12_t4_verdikt",
                 "data": {"verdicts_a": a["verdicts"],
                          "verdicts_b": b["verdicts"],
                          "basis_a": a["final_basis"],
                          "basis_b_sizes": b["basis_sizes"],
                          "expansions_a": a["expansions"],
                          "expansions_b": b["expansions"]},
                 "assert": (all(v == "HALTED" for v in a["verdicts"])
                            and a["final_basis"] == 3
                            and a["expansions"] == []
                            and b["verdicts"][0] == "LOOP"
                            and b["verdicts"][1] == "LOOP"
                            and b["basis_sizes"][0] == 5
                            and b["basis_sizes"][1] == 7
                            and b["expansions"][:2]
                            == ["step1:LOOP", "step2:LOOP"]),
                 "note": ("Arm B: das gelernte Wort wird Programm — "
                          "('identity','x') ist Tautologie -> LOOP -> "
                          "+2 Basis-Terme je LOOP (Turing-innerer Expand "
                          ":241-246 + Composite :132-134); Schritte 3-6 "
                          "DATEN (Pool-Mechanik)")})

    for r in rows:
        r["data"] = {k: (v if not isinstance(v, list) else v[:12])
                     for k, v in r["data"].items()}
    return rows


def main() -> int:
    print("== SP12 — Lexical Grounding: das gelernte Wort wird Programm ==")
    print("Canon: composite.py:108-140/:66-92, turing_machine.py:144-157/:241-246,")
    print("       wittgenstein_machine.py:49-50/:152-246/:342-381")
    print("Prereg tau = 0.85; 5 Lernrunden, noise_level 0.0; seed 42; 6 Schritte")

    l0 = lane_l0_anchor()
    twin = lane_zwilling()
    det = lane_det(twin["arm_a"], twin["arm_b"])
    rows = findings(twin, det, l0)

    print()
    print("BEFUNDE:")
    for r in rows:
        print(f"  {r['id']:<26} {'PASS' if r['assert'] else 'FAIL'}")
    print()
    print("NOTIZEN:")
    for r in rows:
        print(f"  - {r['id']}: {r['note']}; "
              + "; ".join(f"{k}={v}" for k, v in r["data"].items()))

    fails = [r for r in rows if not r["assert"]]
    print()
    b = twin["arm_b"]
    print("Zwilling (seed 42, 6 Schritte, obj='spiegel'):")
    print("  Arm A verdicts:", twin["verdicts_a"], "| final_basis",
          twin["basis_a"], "| expansions", twin["expansions_a"])
    print("  Arm B verdicts:", twin["verdicts_b"], "| final_basis",
          twin["basis_b"], "| expansions", twin["expansions_b"])
    print("  Arm B basis-Verlauf:", twin["basis_b_sizes"])
    print("  Arm B lexical:", b["rows"][0]["lexical_word"], "->",
          b["rows"][0]["lexical_ast"])
    print("  resonance_after:", twin["arm_b_meta"]["resonance_after"])
    print()
    print(f"BILANZ: {len(rows) - len(fails)} PASS; {len(fails)} FEHLGESCHLAGEN"
          f"{', unverwartet' if fails else ''}; 0 OFFEN")
    print(f"JSON: {JSON_PATH}")

    payload = {"l0": l0, "twin": twin, "det": det, "findings": rows,
               "all_pass": not fails, "open": []}
    with open(JSON_PATH, "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        json.dump(payload, fh, indent=2, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False)

    if fails:
        print("ROT — Bands verletzt (Daten, kein Band-Schieben): "
              + ", ".join(r["id"] for r in fails))
        return 1
    print("GRUEN — Semantische Kausalität geschlossen (das gelernte Wort wird Programm).")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
